bsize(b): return (max(b[2],b[5]) - min(b[2],b[5])) * 100\n","def bhi(b):   return max(b[2], b[5])\n","def blo(b):   return min(b[2], b[5])\n","def O(b):     return b[2]\n","def H(b):     return b[3]\n","def L(b):     return b[4]\n","def C(b):     return b[5]\n","\n","\n","def _check_high(bars, li, rj, mid, R55, thick_exc=False, right2_exc=False):\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจ Swing High\n","    thick_exc   = ใช้ ซ้าย1+ขวา1 แทน ซ้าย3+ขวา2 (exception แท่งหนา)\n","    right2_exc  = ถ้า Right2 ไม่มีในข้อมูล ให้ผ่านทันที (Right2 exception)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    left_range  = [li-1] if thick_exc else [li-3, li-2, li-1]\n","    right_range = [rj+1] if thick_exc else [rj+1, rj+2]\n","\n","    for k in left_range:\n","        if k < 0: continue\n","        b = bars[k]\n","        if O(b) >= mid or C(b) >= mid: return False\n","\n","    for k in right_range:\n","        if k >= len(bars):\n","            return right2_exc   # ← Right2 exception\n","        b = bars[k]\n","        if O(b) >= mid or C(b) >= mid: return False\n","\n","    return True\n","\n","\n","def _check_low(bars, li, rj, mid, R55, thick_exc=False, right2_exc=False):\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจ Swing Low (Mirror จาก Swing High)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    left_range  = [li-1] if thick_exc else [li-3, li-2, li-1]\n","    right_range = [rj+1] if thick_exc else [rj+1, rj+2]\n","\n","    for k in left_range:\n","        if k < 0: continue\n","        b = bars[k]\n","        if O(b) <= mid or C(b) <= mid: return False\n","\n","    for k in right_range:\n","        if k >= len(bars):\n","            return right2_exc\n","        b = bars[k]\n","        if O(b) <= mid or C(b) <= mid: return False\n","\n","    return True\n","\n","\n","def scan_swings(bars, R55,\n","                cur_H=None, hl_zone_hi=None,   # BUY: HL Right2 exception\n","                cur_L=None, lh_zone_lo=None):   # SELL: LL Right2 exception\n","    \"\"\"\n","    สแกนหา Swing High และ Swing Low ทั้งหมดใน bars\n","    พร้อมข้อยกเว้นทั้ง 2 แบบ:\n","      1. แท่งหนา > 5%R55 → ซ้าย1+ขวา1\n","      2. Right2 ไม่มีในข้อมูล แต่ราคาแตะ Entry Zone → ผ่านทันที\n","    \"\"\"\n","    thresh1  = R55 * 0.01  # 1%R55  — เงื่อนไขขั้นต่ำแท่งคู่\n","    thresh5  = R55 * 0.10  # 10%R55  — แท่งหนา exception\n","\n","    highs, lows = [], []\n","    seen_h, seen_l = set(), set()\n","\n","    def try_sh(li, rj, c1, c3):\n","        key = (c1[0], c3[0])\n","        if key in seen_h: return\n","        if abs(C(c1) - O(c3)) * 100 > pair_thresh: return\n","        if bsize(c1) < thresh1 or bsize(c3) < thresh1: return\n","        mid = (C(c1) + O(c3)) / 2\n","\n","        # แท่งหนา exception\n","        thick = bsize(c1) > thresh5 or bsize(c3) > thresh5\n","\n","        # Right2 exception (BUY: HL แตะ HL zone)\n","        r2_exc = bool(\n","            cur_L is not None and hl_zone_hi is not None and cur_L <= hl_zone_hi\n","        )\n","\n","        if _check_high(bars, li, rj, mid, R55, thick_exc=thick, right2_exc=r2_exc):\n","            seen_h.add(key)\n","            highs.append({\n","                \"body_hi\": max(bhi(c1), bhi(c3)),\n","                \"mid\": mid,\n","                \"times\": [bars[li][1], bars[rj][1]],\n","                \"bar_nums\": [bars[li][0], bars[rj][0]],\n","                \"thick\": thick,\n","            })\n","\n","    def try_sl(li, rj, c1, c3):\n","        key = (c1[0], c3[0])\n","        if key in seen_l: return\n","        if abs(C(c1) - O(c3)) * 100 > pair_thresh: return\n","        if bsize(c1) < thresh1 or bsize(c3) < thresh1: return\n","        mid = (C(c1) + O(c3)) / 2\n","\n","        # แท่งหนา exception\n","        thick = bsize(c1) > thresh5 or bsize(c3) > thresh5\n","\n","        # Right2 exception (SELL: LL แตะ LH zone)\n","        r2_exc = bool(\n","            cur_H is not None and lh_zone_lo is not None and cur_H >= lh_zone_lo\n","        )\n","\n","        if _check_low(bars, li, rj, mid, R55, thick_exc=thick, right2_exc=r2_exc):\n","            seen_l.add(key)\n","            lows.append({\n","                \"body_lo\": min(blo(c1), blo(c3)),\n","                \"mid\": mid,\n","                \"times\": [bars[li][1], bars[rj][1]],\n","                \"bar_nums\": [bars[li][0], bars[rj][0]],\n","                \"thick\": thick,\n","            })\n","\n","    for i in range(len(bars) - 1):\n","        j = i + 1\n","        bi, bj = bars[i], bars[j]\n","        pair_thresh = max(R55 * 0.01, 100)   # 1%R55 หรือ 100pip แล้วแต่มากกว่า\n","        if abs(C(bi) - O(bj)) * 100 > pair_thresh: continue\n","\n","        bid = bsize(bi) < thresh1\n","        bjd = bsize(bj) < thresh1\n","\n","        if not bid and not bjd:\n","            try_sh(i, j, bi, bj)\n","            try_sl(i, j, bi, bj)\n","        elif not bid and bjd:   # bj=Doji → ขยายขวา\n","            if j + 1 < len(bars):\n","                try_sh(i, j+1, bi, bars[j+1])\n","                try_sl(i, j+1, bi, bars[j+1])\n","        elif bid and not bjd:   # bi=Doji → ขยายซ้าย\n","            if i - 1 >= 0:\n","                try_sh(i-1, j, bars[i-1], bj)\n","                try_sl(i-1, j, bars[i-1], bj)\n","        # ทั้งคู่ Doji → ข้าม\n","\n","    return highs, lows\n","\n","\n","\n","# ══ xauusd_signal v4.61 ══\n","# เงื่อนไขใหม่ภูเขา:\n","#   ความสมมาตร X/Y ∈ [0.5, 1.5] (X=แท่งขาขึ้น Y=แท่งขาลง)\n","#   ขนาดภูเขา (X+Y) < 10 แท่ง → ไม่เล่น\n","\n","from datetime import datetime, time as dtime\n","\n","\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Time & Gap Rules\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","\n","def parse_bar_time(time_str: str) -> datetime | None:\n","    \"\"\"Parse timestamp จาก bar เช่น '2026-04-21 08:15' หรือ '08:15'\"\"\"\n","    for fmt in ('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M', '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S', '%H:%M'):\n","        try:\n","            return datetime.strptime(time_str.strip(), fmt)\n","        except ValueError:\n","            continue\n","    return None\n","\n","\n","def is_near_market_close(time_str: str, close_hour: int = 21,\n","                         close_minute: int = 0, buffer_minutes: int = 120) -> bool:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจว่าเวลาปัจจุบันอยู่ภายใน buffer_minutes ก่อนตลาดปิดวันศุกร์หรือไม่\n","    ป้องกันการถือ Position ข้ามสัปดาห์\n","    คืน True = ห้ามเปิด Order (เฉพาะวันศุกร์เท่านั้น)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    dt = parse_bar_time(time_str)\n","    if dt is None:\n","        return False\n","    # ตรวจเฉพาะวันศุกร์ (weekday() == 4)\n","    if dt.weekday() != 4:\n","        return False\n","    close_dt = dt.replace(hour=close_hour, minute=close_minute, second=0)\n","    diff = (close_dt - dt).total_seconds() / 60\n","    return 0 <= diff <= buffer_minutes\n","\n","\n","def check_opening_gap(bars: list, R55: float,\n","                      gap_threshold_pct: float = 0.20,\n","                      wait_bars: int = 80) -> int:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจ Gap เมื่อเปิดตลาด\n","    คืน: จำนวน bar index ที่ต้องรอ (0 = เริ่มได้ทันที)\n","\n","    bars: list ของ OHLC เรียงจากเก่า→ใหม่ (ต้องมี timestamp ที่มีวันที่)\n","    R55: Range 55 แท่ง (pip)\n","    gap_threshold_pct: สัดส่วน Gap ขั้นต่ำที่ต้องรอ (default 20%)\n","    wait_bars: จำนวนแท่งที่ต้องรอ (default 80)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    if len(bars) < 2:\n","        return 0\n","\n","    gap_threshold = R55 * gap_threshold_pct / 100  # USD\n","\n","    # หาจุดเปลี่ยนวัน\n","    gap_bar_idx = None\n","    for i in range(1, len(bars)):\n","        dt_prev = parse_bar_time(bars[i-1].time)\n","        dt_curr = parse_bar_time(bars[i].time)\n","        if dt_prev is None or dt_curr is None:\n","            continue\n","        if dt_curr.date() != dt_prev.date():\n","            # พบจุดเปลี่ยนวัน\n","            gap = abs(bars[i].open - bars[i-1].close)\n","            if gap > gap_threshold:\n","                gap_bar_idx = i\n","                break\n","\n","    if gap_bar_idx is None:\n","        return 0\n","\n","    return gap_bar_idx + wait_bars  # bar index ที่เริ่มวิเคราะห์ได้\n","\n","\n","\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Data Types\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","\n","@dataclass\n","class OHLC:\n","    time:  str\n","    open:  float\n","    high:  float\n","    low:   float\n","    close: float\n","    bar_num: int = 0  # ถ้าไม่ระบุ จะถูก assign ตาม index\n","\n","    @property\n","    def body_hi(self): return max(self.open, self.close)\n","    @property\n","    def body_lo(self): return min(self.open, self.close)\n","    @property\n","    def body_size(self): return (self.body_hi - self.body_lo) * 100  # pip\n","\n","    def to_tuple(self):\n","        \"\"\"แปลงเป็น tuple สำหรับ swing_v414\"\"\"\n","        return (self.bar_num, self.time, self.open, self.high, self.low, self.close)\n","\n","\n","@dataclass\n","class Signal:\n","    pattern:    str           # 'DOWNTREND' | 'MOUNTAIN' | 'MOUNTAIN_R2' | 'UPTREND'\n","    direction:  str           # 'BUY' | 'SELL'\n","    quality:    str           # '100%✅' | '~60%⚠️'\n","    entry:      float\n","    sl:         float\n","    sl_name:    str           # 'SL1' | 'SL2' | 'SL3' | 'SL3.5'\n","    tp_order:   float         # ราคาวาง order จริง\n","    tp_ref:     float         # ราคาใช้ตรวจ R:R\n","    tp_name:    str           # 'TP1' | 'TP2' | 'TP3'\n","    rr:         float\n","    risk_pip:   float\n","    reward_pip: float\n","    lot:        float = 0.0\n","    R55:        float = 0.0\n","    swing_bar_nums: set  = field(default_factory=set)  # bar_nums ของ Swing Point ที่ใช้เป็น entry\n","    details:    dict  = field(default_factory=dict)\n","\n","\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Helpers\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","\n","_TP3_REF_BUF  =  0.10   # 10pip\n","_TP3_ORD_BUF  =  0.60   # 60pip  (spread buffer)\n","_BUF100       =  1.00   # 100pip\n","_CROSS_BUF    =  1.00   # ±100pip สำหรับตรวจพาดผ่าน\n","\n","def _q(pct: float) -> str:\n","    return \"100%✅\" if pct < 30 else \"~60%⚠️\" if pct < 50 else \"❌\"\n","\n","def _bars_to_tuples(bars: list[OHLC]) -> list[tuple]:\n","    out = []\n","    for i, b in enumerate(bars):\n","        bn = b.bar_num if b.bar_num else i + 1\n","        out.append((bn, b.time, b.open, b.high, b.low, b.close))\n","    return out\n","\n","def _O(t): return t[2]\n","def _H(t): return t[3]\n","def _L(t): return t[4]\n","def _C(t): return t[5]\n","\n","\n","def _pick_tp(entry: float, tps: list[tuple], sl: float, direction: str) -> tuple:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    เลือก TP ที่ R:R ใกล้เคียง 1.0 ที่สุด แต่ไม่น้อยกว่า 1.0\n","    direction: 'BUY' หรือ 'SELL'\n","    tps: list of (tp_ref, tp_order, tp_name)\n","    คืน (tp_ref, tp_order, tp_name, rr) ของ TP ที่เลือก\n","    \"\"\"\n","    risk = abs(entry - sl) * 100\n","    if risk <= 0:\n","        return None\n","\n","    best = None\n","    for tp_ref, tp_ord, tp_name in tps:\n","        reward = abs(entry - tp_ref) * 100\n","        rr = reward / risk\n","        if rr >= 1.0:\n","            if best is None or rr < best[3]:   # ใกล้ 1 ที่สุด\n","                best = (tp_ref, tp_ord, tp_name, rr)\n","\n","    return best  # (tp_ref, tp_order, tp_name, rr) หรือ None\n","\n","\n","def _calc_lot(risk_pip: float, portfolio: float) -> float:\n","    \"\"\"Lot = (portfolio × 10%) ÷ risk_pip\"\"\"\n","    if risk_pip <= 0:\n","        return 0.0\n","    return round((portfolio * 0.10) / risk_pip, 2)\n","\n","\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# SL Calculators\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","\n","def _calc_sl_sell(tech: float, frame_tuples: list[tuple],\n","                  pre_tuples: list[tuple], R55: float) -> tuple[float, str]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    คำนวณ SL สำหรับ SELL (เทรนลง)\n","    tech = body_hi ของ LH\n","    คืน (sl_price, sl_name)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    p15 = R55 * 0.15 / 100\n","    p10 = R55 * 0.10 / 100\n","    p5  = R55 * 0.05 / 100\n","    buf = _CROSS_BUF\n","\n","    sl1 = tech + p15\n","    max_h_frame = max(_H(b) for b in frame_tuples)\n","\n","    # ① ไม่พ้นไส้ → SL2\n","    if sl1 <= max_h_frame:\n","        sl2 = max_h_frame + p10\n","        cross2 = [b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b) - buf) <= sl2 <= (_H(b) + buf)]\n","        if len(cross2) <= 1:\n","            return sl2, \"SL2\"\n","        mhc2 = max(_H(b) for b in cross2)\n","        return mhc2 + p10, \"SL3\"\n","\n","    # ② พ้นไส้ แต่พาดผ่าน ≥2 แท่ง → SL3.5\n","    cross1 = [b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b) - buf) <= sl1 <= (_H(b) + buf)]\n","    if len(cross1) >= 2:\n","        mhc1 = max(_H(b) for b in cross1)\n","        return mhc1 + p5, \"SL3.5\"\n","\n","    return sl1, \"SL1\"\n","\n","\n","def _calc_sl_buy(tech: float, frame_tuples: list[tuple],\n","                 pre_tuples: list[tuple], R55: float) -> tuple[float, str]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    คำนวณ SL สำหรับ BUY (เทรนขึ้น)\n","    tech = body_lo ของ HL\n","    คืน (sl_price, sl_name)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    p15 = R55 * 0.15 / 100\n","    p10 = R55 * 0.10 / 100\n","    p5  = R55 * 0.05 / 100\n","    buf = _CROSS_BUF\n","\n","    sl1 = tech - p15\n","    min_l_frame = min(_L(b) for b in frame_tuples)\n","\n","    # ① ไม่พ้นไส้ → SL2\n","    if sl1 >= min_l_frame:\n","        sl2 = min_l_frame - p10\n","        cross2 = [b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b) - buf) <= sl2 <= (_H(b) + buf)]\n","        if len(cross2) <= 1:\n","            return sl2, \"SL2\"\n","        mlc2 = min(_L(b) for b in cross2)\n","        return mlc2 - p10, \"SL3\"\n","\n","    # ② พ้นไส้ แต่พาดผ่าน ≥2 แท่ง → SL3.5\n","    cross1 = [b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b) - buf) <= sl1 <= (_H(b) + buf)]\n","    if len(cross1) >= 2:\n","        mlc1 = min(_L(b) for b in cross1)\n","        return mlc1 - p5, \"SL3.5\"\n","\n","    return sl1, \"SL1\"\n","\n","\n","def _calc_sl_mountain(base_low: float, base_tuples: list[tuple],\n","                      pre_tuples: list[tuple], R55: float) -> tuple[float, str]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    SL สำหรับภูเขา\n","    SL1 = ไส้ต่ำสุดแท่งคู่ฐาน − 15%R55\n","    ถ้าพาดผ่าน ≥2 แท่ง → SL2\n","    คืน (sl_price, sl_name)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    p15 = R55 * 0.15 / 100\n","    p10 = R55 * 0.10 / 100\n","    buf = _CROSS_BUF\n","\n","    sl1 = base_low - p15  # จุดเทคนิค (body_lo) − 15%R55\n","    cross1 = [b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b) - buf) <= sl1 <= (_H(b) + buf)]\n","    if len(cross1) >= 2:\n","        mlc1 = min(_L(b) for b in cross1)\n","        return mlc1 - p10, \"SL2\"\n","\n","    return sl1, \"SL1\"\n","\n","\n","def _calc_sl_sell_all(tech, frame_tuples, pre_tuples, R55):\n","    p15=R55*0.15/100; p10=R55*0.10/100; p5=R55*0.05/100; buf=_CROSS_BUF\n","    sl1=tech+p15; mh=max(_H(b) for b in frame_tuples) if frame_tuples else sl1; sl2=mh+p10\n","    c2=[b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b)-buf)<=sl2<=(_H(b)+buf)]\n","    sl3=round(max(_H(b) for b in c2)+p10,3) if len(c2)>1 else ''\n","    c1=[b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b)-buf)<=sl1<=(_H(b)+buf)]\n","    sl35=round(max(_H(b) for b in c1)+p5,3) if len(c1)>=2 else ''\n","    return {'SL1':round(sl1,3),'SL2':round(sl2,3),'SL3':sl3,'SL3.5':sl35}\n","\n","def _calc_sl_buy_all(tech, frame_tuples, pre_tuples, R55):\n","    p15=R55*0.15/100; p10=R55*0.10/100; p5=R55*0.05/100; buf=_CROSS_BUF\n","    sl1=tech-p15; ml=min(_L(b) for b in frame_tuples) if frame_tuples else sl1; sl2=ml-p10\n","    c2=[b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b)-buf)<=sl2<=(_H(b)+buf)]\n","    sl3=round(min(_L(b) for b in c2)-p10,3) if len(c2)>1 else ''\n","    c1=[b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b)-buf)<=sl1<=(_H(b)+buf)]\n","    sl35=round(min(_L(b) for b in c1)-p5,3) if len(c1)>=2 else ''\n","    return {'SL1':round(sl1,3),'SL2':round(sl2,3),'SL3':sl3,'SL3.5':sl35}\n","\n","def _calc_sl_mountain_all(base_tuples, pre_tuples, R55):\n","    \"\"\"ใช้ body_lo (min close/open) เหมือน _calc_sl_mountain\"\"\"\n","    p15=R55*0.15/100; p10=R55*0.10/100; buf=_CROSS_BUF\n","    sl1=min(blo(b) for b in base_tuples)-p15\n","    c1=[b for b in pre_tuples if (_L(b)-buf)<=sl1<=(_H(b)+buf)]\n","    sl2=round(min(blo(b) for b in c1)-p10,3) if len(c1)>=2 else ''\n","    return {'SL1':round(sl1,3),'SL2':sl2,'SL3':'','SL3.5':''}\n","\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Pattern Detectors\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","\n","\n","\n","\n","\n","\n","def _verify_uptrend_impulse(hs: list, ls: list, window_t: list, R55: float, L55: float, H55: float) -> bool:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    เทรนขึ้น Impulse:\n","    1. HH/HL structure\n","    2. Impulse: bearish 3-10 แท่งต่อเนื่อง รวม > 50% R55 แต่ละแท่ง body ≥ 3% R55\n","    3. Consolidation: ≤ 30% R55 อยู่เหนือ 60% R55\n","    \"\"\"\n","    if len(hs) < 2 or len(ls) < 2:\n","        return False\n","    if not (hs[-1]['body_hi'] > hs[-2]['body_hi'] and ls[-1]['body_lo'] > ls[-2]['body_lo']):\n","        return False\n","    thresh3 = R55 * 0.03 / 100\n","    found_impulse = False\n","    for i in range(len(window_t)):\n","        total = 0.0; cnt = 0\n","        for j in range(i, min(i+10, len(window_t))):\n","            b = window_t[j]\n","            body = (max(_O(b),_C(b)) - min(_O(b),_C(b))) * 100\n","            if _C(b) > _O(b) and body >= thresh3*100:\n","                total += body; cnt += 1\n","                if cnt >= 3 and total > R55 * 0.50:\n","                    found_impulse = True; break\n","            else:\n","                break\n","        if found_impulse: break\n","    if not found_impulse:\n","        return False\n","    line_60 = L55 + (H55 - L55) * 0.60\n","    hl_lo = ls[-1]['body_lo']; hh_hi = hs[-1]['body_hi']\n","    if hl_lo < line_60: return False\n","    if (hh_hi - hl_lo) * 100 > R55 * 0.30: return False\n","    return True\n","\n","\n","    if not (hs[-1]['body_hi'] > hs[-2]['body_hi'] and ls[-1]['body_lo'] > ls[-2]['body_lo']):\n","        return False\n","    thresh1 = R55 * 0.01 / 100\n","    for i in range(len(window_t)):\n","        total = 0.0; cnt = 0\n","        for j in range(i, min(i+10, len(window_t))):\n","            b = window_t[j]\n","            body = (max(_O(b),_C(b)) - min(_O(b),_C(b))) * 100\n","            if _C(b) > _O(b) and body >= thresh1*100:\n","                total += body; cnt += 1\n","                if cnt >= 2 and total > R55 * 0.30:\n","                    return True\n","            else:\n","                break\n","    return False\n","\n","\n","def _verify_downtrend_impulse(hs: list, ls: list, window_t: list, R55: float, L55: float, H55: float) -> bool:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    เทรนลง Impulse:\n","    1. LH/LL structure\n","    2. Impulse: bearish 3-10 แท่งต่อเนื่อง รวม > 50% R55 แต่ละแท่ง body ≥ 3% R55\n","    3. Consolidation: ≤ 30% R55 อยู่ต่ำกว่า 40% R55\n","    \"\"\"\n","    if len(hs) < 2 or len(ls) < 2:\n","        return False\n","    if not (hs[-1]['body_hi'] < hs[-2]['body_hi'] and ls[-1]['body_lo'] < ls[-2]['body_lo']):\n","        return False\n","    thresh3 = R55 * 0.03 / 100\n","    found_impulse = False\n","    for i in range(len(window_t)):\n","        total = 0.0; cnt = 0\n","        for j in range(i, min(i+10, len(window_t))):\n","            b = window_t[j]\n","            body = (max(_O(b),_C(b)) - min(_O(b),_C(b))) * 100\n","            if _C(b) < _O(b) and body >= thresh3*100:\n","                total += body; cnt += 1\n","                if cnt >= 3 and total > R55 * 0.50:\n","                    found_impulse = True; break\n","            else:\n","                break\n","        if found_impulse: break\n","    if not found_impulse:\n","        return False\n","    line_40 = L55 + (H55 - L55) * 0.40\n","    lh_hi = hs[-1]['body_hi']; ll_lo = ls[-1]['body_lo']\n","    if lh_hi > line_40: return False\n","    if (lh_hi - ll_lo) * 100 > R55 * 0.30: return False\n","    return True\n","\n","\n","    if not (hs[-1]['body_hi'] < hs[-2]['body_hi'] and ls[-1]['body_lo'] < ls[-2]['body_lo']):\n","        return False\n","    thresh1 = R55 * 0.01 / 100\n","    for i in range(len(window_t)):\n","        total = 0.0; cnt = 0\n","        for j in range(i, min(i+10, len(window_t))):\n","            b = window_t[j]\n","            body = (max(_O(b),_C(b)) - min(_O(b),_C(b))) * 100\n","            if _C(b) < _O(b) and body >= thresh1*100:\n","                total += body; cnt += 1\n","                if cnt >= 2 and total > R55 * 0.30:\n","                    return True\n","            else:\n","                break\n","    return False\n","\n","\n","def _check_prev_trend(all_t: list[tuple], window_start_bar: int,\n","                      R55: float, direction: str) -> bool:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจ 25 แท่งก่อน window ปัจจุบัน ว่ายังเป็นเทรนต่อเนื่องหรือไม่\n","    direction: 'down' หรือ 'up'\n","    คืน True = เทรนต่อเนื่อง ✅ / False = ไม่ต่อเนื่อง ❌\n","    \"\"\"\n","    idx = next((i for i, b in enumerate(all_t) if b[0] == window_start_bar), None)\n","    if idx is None or idx < 2:\n","        return True\n","\n","    prev_bars = all_t[max(0, idx - 25):idx]\n","    if len(prev_bars) < 5:\n","        return True\n","\n","    H_prev = max(_H(b) for b in prev_bars)\n","    L_prev = min(_L(b) for b in prev_bars)\n","    R_sw = (H_prev - L_prev) * 100  # R55 ของ 25 แท่งก่อนหน้า\n","    if R_sw == 0:\n","        return True\n","\n","    thresh_30 = R_sw * 0.30 / 100  # 30% R55_sw (USD)\n","\n","    # ① ตรวจ LH/LL หรือ HH/HL\n","    hs_p, ls_p = scan_swings(prev_bars, R_sw)\n","    if len(hs_p) < 2 or len(ls_p) < 2:\n","        return True\n","\n","    if direction == 'down':\n","        # LH/LL structure\n","        if not (hs_p[-1]['body_hi'] < hs_p[-2]['body_hi'] and\n","                ls_p[-1]['body_lo'] < ls_p[-2]['body_lo']):\n","            return False\n","        # ② ไม่มีแท่ง Bullish body > 30% R55_sw\n","        for b in prev_bars:\n","            body = (max(_O(b), _C(b)) - min(_O(b), _C(b)))\n","            if _C(b) > _O(b) and body > thresh_30:\n","                return False\n","    else:  # up\n","        # HH/HL structure\n","        if not (hs_p[-1]['body_hi'] > hs_p[-2]['body_hi'] and\n","                ls_p[-1]['body_lo'] > ls_p[-2]['body_lo']):\n","            return False\n","        # ② ไม่มีแท่ง Bearish body > 30% R55_sw\n","        for b in prev_bars:\n","            body = (max(_O(b), _C(b)) - min(_O(b), _C(b)))\n","            if _C(b) < _O(b) and body > thresh_30:\n","                return False\n","\n","    return True\n","\n","def _detect_downtrend(window_t: list[tuple], all_t: list[tuple],\n","                      R55: float, H55: float, L55: float,\n","                      cur: tuple,\n","                      used_swing_bars: Optional[set] = None) -> Optional[Signal]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจเทรนลง → SELL\n","    กรอบบน  = LH สูงสุดที่ยังอยู่ต่ำกว่า 50%R55\n","    กรอบล่าง = LL ต่ำสุด\n","    \"\"\"\n","    hs, ls = scan_swings(window_t, R55)\n","\n","    # ตรวจโครงสร้าง DOWNTREND_IMPULSE\n","    if _verify_downtrend_impulse(hs, ls, window_t, R55, L55, H55):\n","        pattern_name = 'DOWNTREND_IMPULSE'\n","    else:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ตรวจ 25 แท่งก่อนหน้าว่าเป็นเทรนลงต่อเนื่องหรือไม่\n","    window_start_bar = window_t[0][0]\n","    if not _check_prev_trend(all_t, window_start_bar, R55, 'down'):\n","        return None\n","\n","    line_50 = L55 + (H55 - L55) * 0.50\n","\n","    # กรอบล่าง = LL ต่ำสุด\n","    ll_sw  = min(ls, key=lambda s: s['body_lo'])\n","    ll_lo  = ll_sw['body_lo']\n","\n","    # กรอบบน = LH สูงสุดที่ body_hi < 50%R55\n","    # กรองจุดที่เคยใช้ entry ไปแล้วออก\n","    lh_candidates = [s for s in hs if s['body_hi'] < line_50]\n","    if used_swing_bars:\n","        lh_candidates = [s for s in lh_candidates\n","                         if not any(bn in used_swing_bars for bn in s['bar_nums'])]\n","    if not lh_candidates:\n","        return None\n","    lh_sw  = max(lh_candidates, key=lambda s: s['body_hi'])  # LH สูงสุดที่ < 50%R55\n","    lh_hi  = lh_sw['body_hi']\n","\n","    # ตรวจ quality %Range\n","    pct_lh = (lh_hi - L55) / (H55 - L55) * 100\n","    pct_ll = (ll_lo - L55) / (H55 - L55) * 100\n","    if pct_lh >= 50 or pct_ll >= 50:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # Rebound = LH − LL\n","    rb_pct = (lh_hi - ll_lo) * 100 / R55 * 100\n","    if not (10 <= rb_pct <= 40):\n","        return None\n","\n","    # Entry Zone = กรอบบน ± 100pip\n","    zone_lo = lh_hi - _BUF100\n","    zone_hi = lh_hi + _BUF100\n","\n","    # ไส้เทียนต้องแตะ Zone\n","    if _H(cur) < zone_lo:\n","        return None\n","\n","    entry = zone_lo  # ขอบล่างของ Zone\n","\n","    # SL — กรอบ LH → LL\n","    lh_b0 = lh_sw['bar_nums'][0]\n","    ll_b1 = ll_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    bmin, bmax = min(lh_b0, ll_b1), max(lh_b0, ll_b1)\n","    frame_t = [b for b in all_t if bmin <= b[0] <= bmax] or \\\n","              [b for b in all_t if b[0] in lh_sw['bar_nums']]\n","\n","    fi = next((i for i, b in enumerate(all_t) if b[0] == frame_t[0][0]), 0)\n","    pre_t = all_t[max(0, fi - 30):fi]\n","\n","    sl, sl_name = _calc_sl_sell(lh_hi, frame_t, pre_t, R55)\n","    risk = abs(sl - entry) * 100\n","    if risk <= 0:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # TP วัดจาก entry → กรอบล่าง (LL)\n","    dist  = entry - ll_lo\n","    tp1   = entry - dist * 0.35\n","    tp2   = entry - dist * 0.50\n","    tp3r  = ll_lo + _TP3_REF_BUF\n","    tp3o  = ll_lo + _TP3_ORD_BUF\n","\n","    picked = _pick_tp(entry, [(tp1, tp1, 'TP1'), (tp2, tp2, 'TP2'),\n","                               (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3')], sl, 'SELL')\n","    if picked is None:\n","        rw3 = (entry - tp3r) * 100\n","        sl_adj = entry + rw3 / 100\n","        picked = (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3(adj)', 1.0)\n","        sl = sl_adj\n","        risk = rw3\n","\n","    tp_ref, tp_ord, tp_name, rr = picked\n","\n","    # Quality\n","    q_lh = \"100%✅\" if pct_lh < 30 else \"~60%⚠️\" if pct_lh < 50 else \"❌\"\n","    q_ll = \"100%✅\" if pct_ll < 30 else \"~60%⚠️\" if pct_ll < 50 else \"❌\"\n","    quality = \"100%✅\" if \"100%\" in q_lh and \"100%\" in q_ll else \"~60%⚠️\"\n","\n","    return Signal(\n","        pattern   = pattern_name,\n","        direction = 'SELL',\n","        quality   = quality,\n","        entry     = entry,\n","        sl        = sl,\n","        sl_name   = sl_name,\n","        tp_order  = tp_ord,\n","        tp_ref    = tp_ref,\n","        tp_name   = tp_name,\n","        rr        = rr,\n","        risk_pip  = abs(sl - entry) * 100,\n","        reward_pip= abs(entry - tp_ref) * 100,\n","        R55       = R55,\n","        swing_bar_nums = set(lh_sw['bar_nums']),\n","        details   = {\n","            'lh': lh_sw, 'll': ll_sw,\n","            'lh_hi': lh_hi, 'll_lo': ll_lo,\n","            'pct_lh': pct_lh, 'pct_ll': pct_ll,\n","            'rebound': rb_pct,\n","            'zone_lo': zone_lo, 'zone_hi': zone_hi,\n","            'tp1': tp1, 'tp2': tp2, 'tp3_ref': tp3r,\n","        }\n","    )\n","\n","\n","def _detect_uptrend(window_t: list[tuple], all_t: list[tuple],\n","                    R55: float, H55: float, L55: float,\n","                    cur: tuple,\n","                    used_swing_bars: Optional[set] = None) -> Optional[Signal]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจเทรนขึ้น → BUY\n","    กรอบล่าง = HL ต่ำสุดที่ยังอยู่เหนือ 50%R55\n","    กรอบบน  = HH สูงสุด\n","    \"\"\"\n","    hs, ls = scan_swings(window_t, R55)\n","\n","    # ตรวจโครงสร้าง UPTREND_IMPULSE\n","    if _verify_uptrend_impulse(hs, ls, window_t, R55, L55, H55):\n","        pattern_name = 'UPTREND_IMPULSE'\n","    else:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ตรวจ 25 แท่งก่อนหน้าว่าเป็นเทรนขึ้นต่อเนื่องหรือไม่\n","    window_start_bar = window_t[0][0]\n","    if not _check_prev_trend(all_t, window_start_bar, R55, 'up'):\n","        return None\n","\n","    line_50 = L55 + (H55 - L55) * 0.50\n","\n","    # กรอบบน = HH สูงสุด\n","    hh_sw = max(hs, key=lambda s: s['body_hi'])\n","    hh_hi = hh_sw['body_hi']\n","\n","    # กรอบล่าง = HL ต่ำสุดที่ body_lo > 50%R55\n","    # กรองจุดที่เคยใช้ entry ไปแล้วออก\n","    hl_candidates = [s for s in ls if s['body_lo'] > line_50]\n","    if used_swing_bars:\n","        hl_candidates = [s for s in hl_candidates\n","                         if not any(bn in used_swing_bars for bn in s['bar_nums'])]\n","    if not hl_candidates:\n","        return None\n","    hl_sw  = min(hl_candidates, key=lambda s: s['body_lo'])\n","    hl_lo  = hl_sw['body_lo']\n","\n","    # ตรวจ quality %Range\n","    pct_hl = (hl_lo - L55) / (H55 - L55) * 100\n","    pct_hh = (hh_hi - L55) / (H55 - L55) * 100\n","    if pct_hl < 50 or pct_hh < 50:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # Rebound = HH − HL\n","    rb_pct = (hh_hi - hl_lo) * 100 / R55 * 100\n","    if not (10 <= rb_pct <= 40):\n","        return None\n","\n","    # Entry Zone = กรอบล่าง ± 100pip\n","    zone_hi = hl_lo + _BUF100\n","    zone_lo = hl_lo - _BUF100\n","\n","    # ไส้เทียนต้องแตะ Zone\n","    if _L(cur) > zone_hi:\n","        return None\n","\n","    entry = zone_hi  # ขอบบนของ Zone\n","\n","    # SL — กรอบ HL → HH\n","    hl_b0 = hl_sw['bar_nums'][0]\n","    hh_b1 = hh_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    bmin, bmax = min(hl_b0, hh_b1), max(hl_b0, hh_b1)\n","    frame_t = [b for b in all_t if bmin <= b[0] <= bmax] or \\\n","              [b for b in all_t if b[0] in hl_sw['bar_nums']]\n","\n","    fi = next((i for i, b in enumerate(all_t) if b[0] == frame_t[0][0]), 0)\n","    pre_t = all_t[max(0, fi - 30):fi]\n","\n","    sl, sl_name = _calc_sl_buy(hl_lo, frame_t, pre_t, R55)\n","    risk = (entry - sl) * 100\n","    if risk <= 0:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # TP วัดจาก entry → HH\n","    dist  = hh_hi - entry\n","    tp1   = entry + dist * 0.35\n","    tp2   = entry + dist * 0.50\n","    tp3r  = hh_hi - _TP3_REF_BUF\n","    tp3o  = hh_hi - _TP3_ORD_BUF\n","\n","    picked = _pick_tp(entry, [(tp1, tp1, 'TP1'), (tp2, tp2, 'TP2'),\n","                               (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3')], sl, 'BUY')\n","    if picked is None:\n","        rw3 = (tp3r - entry) * 100\n","        sl_adj = entry - rw3 / 100\n","        picked = (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3(adj)', 1.0)\n","        sl = sl_adj\n","        risk = rw3\n","\n","    tp_ref, tp_ord, tp_name, rr = picked\n","\n","    # Quality\n","    q_hl = \"100%✅\" if pct_hl < 30 else \"~60%⚠️\" if pct_hl < 50 else \"❌\"\n","    q_hh = \"100%✅\" if (100 - pct_hh) < 30 else \"~60%⚠️\"\n","    quality = \"100%✅\" if \"100%\" in q_hl and \"100%\" in q_hh else \"~60%⚠️\"\n","\n","    return Signal(\n","        pattern   = pattern_name,\n","        direction = 'BUY',\n","        quality   = quality,\n","        entry     = entry,\n","        sl        = sl,\n","        sl_name   = sl_name,\n","        tp_order  = tp_ord,\n","        tp_ref    = tp_ref,\n","        tp_name   = tp_name,\n","        rr        = rr,\n","        risk_pip  = abs(entry - sl) * 100,\n","        reward_pip= abs(tp_ref - entry) * 100,\n","        R55       = R55,\n","        swing_bar_nums = set(hl_sw['bar_nums']),\n","        details   = {\n","            'hl': hl_sw, 'hh': hh_sw,\n","            'hl_lo': hl_lo, 'hh_hi': hh_hi,\n","            'pct_hl': pct_hl, 'pct_hh': pct_hh,\n","            'rebound': rb_pct,\n","            'zone_lo': zone_lo, 'zone_hi': zone_hi,\n","            'tp1': tp1, 'tp2': tp2, 'tp3_ref': tp3r,\n","        }\n","    )\n","\n","\n","def _detect_mountain(window_t: list[tuple], all_t: list[tuple],\n","                     R55: float, cur: tuple,\n","                     prev_entry: Optional[float] = None,\n","                     prev_entry_bar: Optional[int] = None,\n","                     base_lo_r1: Optional[float] = None,\n","                     used_swing_bars: Optional[set] = None) -> Optional[Signal]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตรวจภูเขา → BUY\n","    prev_entry      = entry ของรอบ 1 (ถ้ามี → ตรวจรอบ 2)\n","    prev_entry_bar  = bar_num ที่ entry รอบ 1\n","    base_lo_r1      = body_lo ฐานซ้าย รอบ 1 (สำหรับตรวจเงื่อนไขรอบ 2)\n","    used_swing_bars = bar_nums ที่เคยใช้เป็น entry ไปแล้ว → ไม่เข้าซ้ำ\n","    \"\"\"\n","    hs, ls = scan_swings(window_t, R55)\n","    if not hs or not ls:\n","        return None\n","\n","    is_round2 = prev_entry is not None\n","\n","    if not is_round2:\n","        # รอบ 1: ฐาน = LL ต่ำสุด ที่ไม่เคยใช้เป็น entry\n","        candidates = ls if not used_swing_bars else [\n","            s for s in ls if not any(bn in used_swing_bars for bn in s['bar_nums'])\n","        ]\n","        if not candidates:\n","            return None\n","        base_sw = min(candidates, key=lambda s: s['body_lo'])\n","    else:\n","        # รอบ 2: ฐานขวา = SL ที่เกิดหลัง entry รอบ 1\n","        after_entry = [s for s in ls\n","                       if s['bar_nums'][0] > (prev_entry_bar or 0)]\n","        if not after_entry:\n","            return None\n","        base_sw = min(after_entry, key=lambda s: s['body_lo'])\n","\n","    peaks_after = [s for s in hs if s['bar_nums'][0] > base_sw['bar_nums'][-1]]\n","    if not peaks_after:\n","        return None\n","    peak_sw = max(peaks_after, key=lambda s: s['body_hi'])  # สูงที่สุดหลังฐาน\n","\n","    base_lo = base_sw['body_lo']\n","    peak_hi = peak_sw['body_hi']\n","    height  = (peak_hi - base_lo) * 100\n","\n","    # ตรวจว่าฐานนี้ \"ติดกัน\" กับฐานที่เคยใช้ไปหรือไม่\n","    # คือ body_lo ใกล้กัน ≤ Buffer (max 100pip)\n","    buf_check = min(height * 0.10, 100) / 100  # USD\n","    is_adjacent = False\n","    if used_swing_bars:\n","        prev_bases = [s for s in ls if any(bn in used_swing_bars for bn in s['bar_nums'])]\n","        for pb in prev_bases:\n","            if abs(base_lo - pb['body_lo']) <= buf_check:\n","                is_adjacent = True\n","                break\n","\n","    # threshold ความสูง: ปกติ 60% / ภูเขาติดกัน 55%\n","    height_thresh = R55 * 0.55 if is_adjacent else R55 * 0.60\n","    if height < height_thresh:\n","        return None  # ความสูงภูเขา < 60%R55\n","    _need_sym = False  # symmetry ปิด\n","\n","\n","\n","    buf = min(height * 0.05, 100)\n","    zone_lo = base_lo - buf / 100\n","    zone_hi = base_lo + buf / 100\n","\n","    # ── ตรวจความสมมาตร ──────────────────────────────────────────\n","    # X = แท่งขาขึ้น (ฐาน → ยอด)  Y = แท่งขาลง (ยอด → entry)\n","    # max(X,Y) / min(X,Y) < 1.5\n","    X = peak_sw['bar_nums'][0] - base_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    Y = cur[0] - peak_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    # symmetry: ปิดชั่วคราว\n","    # if Y > 0 and X > 0:\n","    #     if max(X, Y) / min(X, Y) >= 1.6:\n","    #         return None\n","\n","    # ── ขนาดภูเขาต้องไม่น้อยกว่า 10 แท่ง ───────────────────────\n","    if (X + Y) < 10:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ── ห้ามมีแท่งที่ Low ≤ base_lo + 10%height ในช่วง 20-80% ────────\n","    thresh_10 = base_lo + (peak_hi - base_lo) * 0.20\n","    all_mid = [b for b in window_t\n","               if base_sw['bar_nums'][-1] < b[0] <= cur[0]]\n","    total_bars = len(all_mid)\n","    if total_bars >= 3:\n","        lo20 = int(total_bars * 0.20)\n","        hi80 = int(total_bars * 0.80)\n","        scan_bars = all_mid[lo20:hi80+1]\n","        for b in scan_bars:\n","            if _L(b) <= thresh_10:\n","                return None  # ราคาลงใกล้ฐานในช่วงกลาง → ❌\n","\n","    # ── ตรวจจำนวนแท่งจากฐาน → entry ไม่เกิน 40 แท่ง ────────────\n","    bars_base_to_entry = cur[0] - base_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    if bars_base_to_entry > 42:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ── ตรวจ Swing High ระหว่างฐาน → Entry Zone ────────────────\n","    # ถ้าจากฐาน → entry ≤ 25 แท่ง → ข้าม SH check\n","    bars_base_to_entry = cur[0] - base_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    if bars_base_to_entry > 25:\n","        peak_bar_last = peak_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","        sh_after_peak = [s for s in hs if s['bar_nums'][0] > peak_bar_last\n","                         and s['bar_nums'][0] <= cur[0]]\n","        if len(sh_after_peak) > 2:\n","            return None\n","\n","    # ── ตรวจแท่งลงแรงเดียว (spike) จากเหนือ 50%R55 ลงมาถึง Zone ──\n","    H55_m = max(_H(b) for b in window_t)\n","    L55_m = min(_L(b) for b in window_t)\n","    line_50 = L55_m + (H55_m - L55_m) * 0.50\n","    peak_bar_last2 = peak_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","    descent_bars2 = [b for b in window_t\n","                     if peak_bar_last2 < b[0] <= cur[0]]\n","    for b in descent_bars2:\n","        if _O(b) > line_50 and _L(b) <= zone_hi:\n","            return None  # แท่ง spike เดียวจากเหนือ 50% ลงถึง Zone → ❌\n","\n","    if not (_L(cur) <= zone_hi and _H(cur) >= zone_lo):\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ── ตรวจทิศทางราคาเข้า Zone ────────────────────────────────\n","    # ต้องวิ่งลงมาแตะ Zone จากข้างบน ไม่ใช่ bounce ขึ้นจากข้างล่าง\n","    cur_idx = next((i for i, b in enumerate(window_t) if b[0] == cur[0]), None)\n","    if cur_idx is not None and cur_idx > 0:\n","        prev_bar = window_t[cur_idx - 1]\n","        if _L(prev_bar) < zone_lo:\n","            return None  # แท่งก่อนหน้าหลุดต่ำกว่า zone แล้ว → ราคาขึ้นมาแตะ ❌\n","\n","    bars_since = cur[0] - peak_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","\n","    entry = min(_H(cur), zone_hi)\n","\n","    # แท่งคู่ฐาน\n","    base_t = [b for b in window_t if b[0] in base_sw['bar_nums']]\n","    if not base_t:\n","        return None\n","\n","    fi = next((i for i, b in enumerate(all_t) if b[0] == base_t[0][0]), 0)\n","    pre_t = all_t[max(0, fi - 30):fi]\n","\n","    sl, sl_name = _calc_sl_mountain(base_lo, base_t, pre_t, R55)\n","    sl_all = _calc_sl_mountain_all(base_t, pre_t, R55)\n","    risk = (entry - sl) * 100\n","    if risk <= 0:\n","        return None\n","\n","    if not is_round2:\n","        # รอบ 1: TP วัดจาก ฐานซ้าย + ความสูง × %\n","        dist = peak_hi - base_lo\n","        tp1  = base_lo + dist * 0.33   # 33% ของความสูง\n","        tp2_base = base_lo + dist * 0.45  # 45% ของความสูง (ก่อน adj)\n","        tp3r = base_lo + dist * 0.80 - _TP3_REF_BUF   # 80% ref\n","        tp3o = base_lo + dist * 0.80 - _TP3_ORD_BUF   # 80% order\n","\n","        # ── ตรวจอุปสรรค (ขาลงภูเขา: Peak → Entry Zone) ─────\n","        peak_bar_last = peak_sw['bar_nums'][-1]\n","        descent_bars = [b for b in window_t\n","                        if peak_bar_last <= b[0] <= cur[0]]\n","\n","        obstacle_bottom = None\n","        p4 = R55 * 0.04 / 100   # 4% R55\n","        p5 = R55 * 0.05 / 100   # 5% R55\n","\n","        # 1. Wick Cluster: upper wick ≥ 3 แท่ง uw > 5%R55 กรอบ tips ≤ 300pip\n","        uw_bars = []\n","        for b in descent_bars:\n","            uw = (_H(b) - max(_O(b), _C(b))) * 100  # upper wick pip\n","            if uw > p5 * 100:\n","                uw_bars.append((_H(b), b))\n","        if len(uw_bars) >= 3:\n","            tips = [h for h, _ in uw_bars]\n","            if (max(tips) - min(tips)) * 100 <= 300:\n","                obs = min(tips) - p4\n","                if obs > tp1:\n","                    obstacle_bottom = obs if obstacle_bottom is None else min(obstacle_bottom, obs)\n","\n","        # 2. Body Cluster: body ≥ 3 แท่ง ใน 10 แท่ง มี common overlap\n","        for i in range(len(descent_bars)):\n","            group = descent_bars[i:i+10]\n","            body_ranges = []\n","            for b in group:\n","                lo = min(_O(b), _C(b)); hi = max(_O(b), _C(b))\n","                body = (hi - lo) * 100\n","                if body >= p5 * 100:\n","                    body_ranges.append((lo, hi))\n","            if len(body_ranges) >= 3:\n","                overlap_lo = max(r[0] for r in body_ranges)\n","                overlap_hi = min(r[1] for r in body_ranges)\n","                if overlap_lo < overlap_hi:\n","                    obs = overlap_lo - p4\n","                    if obs > tp1:\n","                        obstacle_bottom = obs if obstacle_bottom is None else min(obstacle_bottom, obs)\n","\n","        # 3. Swing Low ที่ body_lo อยู่เหนือ 40% ความสูง\n","        #    ตรวจเฉพาะช่วง ฐาน → entry\n","        upper_thresh = base_lo + dist * 0.40\n","        hs_all, ls_all = scan_swings(window_t, R55)\n","        for s in ls_all:\n","            # กรองเฉพาะ SL ที่อยู่ระหว่างฐาน → entry\n","            if s['bar_nums'][0] <= base_sw['bar_nums'][-1]:\n","                continue\n","            if s['bar_nums'][-1] > cur[0]:\n","                continue\n","            if s['body_lo'] > upper_thresh:\n","                # ใช้ไส้เทียนต่ำสุด (Low) ถ้ามี แทนเนื้อเทียน\n","                wick_lo = min(\n","                    _L(b) for b in window_t if b[0] in s['bar_nums']\n","                )\n","                ref_price = min(s['body_lo'], wick_lo)\n","                obs = ref_price - p4\n","                if obs > tp1:\n","                    obstacle_bottom = obs if obstacle_bottom is None else min(obstacle_bottom, obs)\n","\n","        # ตัดสิน TP2\n","        has_obstacle = obstacle_bottom is not None and obstacle_bottom > tp1\n","        if has_obstacle:\n","            if tp2_base < obstacle_bottom:\n","                tp2     = tp2_base\n","                tp2_adj = None\n","                has_obstacle = False\n","            else:\n","                tp2_adj = obstacle_bottom\n","                tp2     = tp2_adj\n","        else:\n","            tp2_adj = None\n","            tp2     = tp2_base\n","\n","        # ── SL = 60% ของความสูงภูเขา ────────────────────────\n","        _sl_dist = (peak_hi - base_lo) * 0.60\n","        sl = entry - _sl_dist\n","        sl_name = 'SL=60%H'\n","        risk = _sl_dist * 100\n","        sl_all = {'SL1': round(sl, 3), 'SL2': '', 'SL3': '', 'SL3.5': ''}\n","\n","        # ── TP = TP3, Trailing SL (รอบ 1) ───────────────────\n","        picked = (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3', abs(tp3r-entry)/max(abs(entry-sl),0.001)*100 if abs(entry-sl)>0 else 1)\n","        pattern_name = 'MOUNTAIN'\n","    else:\n","        # รอบ 2: TP วัดจาก body_hi สูงสุดหลัง entry รอบ 1\n","        after_r1 = [b for b in all_t if b[0] > (prev_entry_bar or 0)]\n","        if not after_r1:\n","            return None\n","        tp_ref_hi = max(_H(b) for b in after_r1)   # ใช้ไส้\n","        tp_ref_body = max(max(_O(b), _C(b)) for b in after_r1)\n","        tp_ref_base = tp_ref_body\n","        dist = tp_ref_base - entry\n","        tp1  = entry + dist / 3\n","        tp2  = entry + dist / 2\n","        tp3r = tp_ref_base - R55 * 0.05 / 100\n","        tp3o = tp_ref_base - R55 * 0.05 / 100 - _TP3_ORD_BUF + _TP3_REF_BUF\n","        pattern_name = 'MOUNTAIN_R2'\n","\n","        # รอบ 2 ใช้ _pick_tp ปกติ\n","        picked = None\n","        if not picked:\n","            picked = _pick_tp(entry, [(tp1, tp1, 'TP1'), (tp2, tp2, 'TP2'),\n","                                       (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3')], sl, 'BUY')\n","        if picked is None:\n","            rw3 = (tp3r - entry) * 100\n","            sl_adj = entry - rw3 / 100\n","            picked = (tp3r, tp3o, 'TP3(adj)', 1.0)\n","            sl = sl_adj\n","            risk = rw3\n","\n","    tp_ref, tp_ord, tp_name, rr = picked\n","    sl_adjusted = sl if tp_name.endswith('(adj)') or 'SL-adj' in tp_name else None\n","\n","    return Signal(\n","        pattern   = pattern_name,\n","        direction = 'BUY',\n","        quality   = '✅' if bars_since <= 40 else '⚠️ นาน',\n","        entry     = entry,\n","        sl        = sl,\n","        sl_name   = sl_name,\n","        tp_order  = tp_ord,\n","        tp_ref    = tp_ref,\n","        tp_name   = tp_name,\n","        rr        = rr,\n","        risk_pip  = abs(entry - sl) * 100,\n","        reward_pip= abs(tp_ref - entry) * 100,\n","        R55       = R55,\n","        swing_bar_nums = set(base_sw['bar_nums']),\n","        details   = {\n","            'base': base_sw, 'peak': peak_sw,\n","            'base_lo': base_lo, 'peak_hi': peak_hi,\n","            'height': height, 'buf': buf,\n","            'zone_lo': zone_lo, 'zone_hi': zone_hi,\n","            'bars_since_peak': bars_since,\n","            'tp1': tp1, 'tp2': tp2, 'tp3_ref': tp3r,\n","            'tp2_base': tp2_base, 'tp2_adj': tp2_adj,\n","            'sl_all': sl_all,\n","            'sl_adjusted': sl_adjusted,\n","        }\n","    )\n","\n","\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Main Entry Point\n","# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","\n","def find_signal(\n","    bars:             list[OHLC],\n","    portfolio:        float = 1000.0,\n","    market_close_hour: int = 21,\n","    market_close_min:  int = 0,\n","    no_trade_before_close_min: int = 60,\n","    used_trend_bars:   Optional[set] = None,  # bar_nums ที่ใช้ใน UPTREND/DOWNTREND\n","    used_mountain_bars: Optional[set] = None,  # bar_nums ที่ใช้ใน MOUNTAIN\n",") -> Optional[Signal]:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    หาสัญญาณ Entry จาก 55 แท่งล่าสุด\n","\n","    Parameters:\n","        bars          : ข้อมูล OHLC เรียงจากเก่า→ใหม่ (ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 55 แท่ง)\n","        portfolio     : เงินทุน USD (ใช้คำนวณ Lot)\n","        market_close_hour/min: เวลาปิดตลาด (default 21:00)\n","        no_trade_before_close_min: ห้ามเปิดก่อนปิดกี่นาที (default 60)\n","        used_trend_bars  : bar_nums ที่เคยใช้ใน UPTREND/DOWNTREND → ไม่เข้าซ้ำ\n","        used_mountain_bars: bar_nums ที่เคยใช้ใน MOUNTAIN → ไม่เข้าซ้ำ\n","\n","    Returns:\n","        Signal หรือ None ถ้าไม่มีสัญญาณ\n","    \"\"\"\n","    if len(bars) < 55:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ── กฎ 1: ห้ามเปิดก่อนตลาดปิด 1 ชั่วโมง ──────────────\n","    cur_bar = bars[-1]\n","    if is_near_market_close(cur_bar.time, market_close_hour,\n","                            market_close_min, no_trade_before_close_min):\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ใช้ 55 แท่งล่าสุด\n","    window = bars[-55:]\n","    cur_bar = window[-1]\n","\n","    # Assign bar_num ถ้าไม่มี\n","    for i, b in enumerate(bars):\n","        if b.bar_num == 0:\n","            b.bar_num = i + 1\n","\n","    window_t = _bars_to_tuples(window)\n","    all_t    = _bars_to_tuples(bars)\n","    cur_t    = window_t[-1]\n","\n","    H55 = max(_H(b) for b in window_t)\n","    L55 = min(_L(b) for b in window_t)\n","    R55 = (H55 - L55) * 100\n","\n","    if R55 == 0:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ── ตรวจอิสระ → เลือก R:R ดีสุด ────────────────────────\n","    candidates = []\n","\n","    # 1. Mountain (อิสระ)\n","    sig_mtn = _detect_mountain(window_t, all_t, R55, cur_t,\n","                               used_swing_bars=used_mountain_bars)\n","    if sig_mtn:\n","        sig_mtn.lot = _calc_lot(sig_mtn.risk_pip, portfolio)\n","        candidates.append(sig_mtn)\n","\n","    # 2. Downtrend — ปิดชั่วคราว\n","    # sig_dn = _detect_downtrend(window_t, all_t, R55, H55, L55, cur_t,\n","    #                            used_swing_bars=used_trend_bars)\n","    # if sig_dn:\n","    #     sig_dn.lot = _calc_lot(sig_dn.risk_pip, portfolio)\n","    #     candidates.append(sig_dn)\n","\n","    # 3. Uptrend — ปิดชั่วคราว\n","    # sig_up = _detect_uptrend(window_t, all_t, R55, H55, L55, cur_t,\n","    #                          used_swing_bars=used_trend_bars)\n","    # if sig_up:\n","    #     sig_up.lot = _calc_lot(sig_up.risk_pip, portfolio)\n","    #     candidates.append(sig_up)\n","\n","    if not candidates:\n","        return None\n","    return max(candidates, key=lambda s: s.rr)\n","\n","\n","def format_signal(sig: Signal, cur_time: str = \"\") -> str:\n","    \"\"\"จัดรูปแบบ output สวยงาม\"\"\"\n","    d = sig.direction\n","    arrow = \"📈\" if d == \"BUY\" else \"📉\"\n","    icon  = \"🏔\" if \"MOUNTAIN\" in sig.pattern else arrow\n","\n","    lines = [\n","        f\"{'═'*52}\",\n","        f\"{icon} {sig.pattern} — {d}  {sig.quality}  {cur_time}\",\n","        f\"{'═'*52}\",\n","        f\"R55      = {sig.R55:.0f} pip\",\n","    ]\n","\n","    det = sig.details\n","    if sig.pattern in ('MOUNTAIN', 'MOUNTAIN_R2'):\n","        lines += [\n","            f\"ฐาน      = {det['base']['times'][0]}–{det['base']['times'][-1]}  {det['base_lo']:.3f}\",\n","            f\"ยอด      = {det['peak']['times'][0]}–{det['peak']['times'][-1]}  {det['peak_hi']:.3f}\",\n","            f\"ความสูง  = {det['height']:.0f}pip ({det['height']/sig.R55*100:.1f}%R55)  {det['bars_since_peak']} แท่งจากยอด\",\n","        ]\n","    else:\n","        if 'lh' in det:\n","            lines += [\n","                f\"LH       = {det['lh']['times'][0]}–{det['lh']['times'][-1]}  {det['lh']['body_hi']:.3f}  {det['pct_lh']:.1f}%\",\n","                f\"LL       = {det['ll']['times'][0]}–{det['ll']['times'][-1]}  {det['ll']['body_lo']:.3f}  {det['pct_ll']:.1f}%\",\n","                f\"Rebound  = {det['rebound']:.1f}%\",\n","            ]\n","        elif 'hl' in det:\n","            lines += [\n","                f\"HL       = {det['hl']['times'][0]}–{det['hl']['times'][-1]}  {det['hl']['body_lo']:.3f}  {det['pct_hl']:.1f}%\",\n","                f\"HH       = {det['hh']['times'][0]}–{det['hh']['times'][-1]}  {det['hh']['body_hi']:.3f}  {det['pct_hh']:.1f}%\",\n","                f\"Rebound  = {det['rebound']:.1f}%\",\n","            ]\n","\n","    lines += [\n","        f\"{'─'*52}\",\n","        f\"Entry    = {sig.entry:.3f}\",\n","        f\"{sig.sl_name:<8} = {sig.sl:.3f}  ({sig.risk_pip:.0f} pip)\",\n","        f\"{'─'*52}\",\n","    ]\n","\n","    for tp_name, tp_val in [('TP1', det.get('tp1')), ('TP2', det.get('tp2')), ('TP3 ref', det.get('tp3_ref'))]:\n","        if tp_val is None:\n","            continue\n","        dist = abs(tp_val - sig.entry) * 100\n","        rr = dist / sig.risk_pip if sig.risk_pip else 0\n","        chosen = \"← เลือก\" if tp_name.replace(' ref','') == sig.tp_name.replace('(adj)','').strip() else \"\"\n","        lines.append(f\"{tp_name:<8} = {tp_val:.3f}  ({dist:.0f}pip)  R:R={rr:.2f}  {chosen}\")\n","\n","    tp_ord_dist = abs(sig.tp_order - sig.entry) * 100\n","    lines += [\n","        f\"TP order = {sig.tp_order:.3f}  ({tp_ord_dist:.0f}pip)  ← วาง order\",\n","        f\"{'─'*52}\",\n","        f\"R:R      = {sig.rr:.2f}\",\n","        f\"Lot      = {sig.lot:.2f}  (portfolio-based)\",\n","        f\"{'═'*52}\",\n","    ]\n","    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n","\n","\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# CSV Loader\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","def load_csv(path):\n","    bars = []\n","    with open(path, newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n","        for i, row in enumerate(csv.reader(f)):\n","            if len(row) < 5: continue\n","            try:\n","                bars.append(OHLC(row[0].strip(), float(row[1]), float(row[2]),\n","                                 float(row[3]), float(row[4]), bar_num=i+1))\n","            except: continue\n","    return bars\n","\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Pattern Labels\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","PTH = {\n","    'DOWNTREND_IMPULSE': 'เทรนลง Impulse',\n","    'UPTREND_IMPULSE':   'เทรนขึ้น Impulse',\n","    'MOUNTAIN':          'ภูเขา',\n","}\n","TCH = {\n","    'DOWNTREND_IMPULSE': 'LH/LL + Bearish 3-10 แท่ง > 50%R55 + Consol ≤ 30% ต่ำกว่า 40%R55',\n","    'UPTREND_IMPULSE':   'HH/HL + Bullish 3-10 แท่ง > 50%R55 + Consol ≤ 30% เหนือ 60%R55',\n","    'MOUNTAIN':          'ภูเขา → BUY ที่ฐานซ้าย ± Buffer',\n","}\n","SKIP_REASON = {\n","    'NEAR_CLOSE': '⏸ ใกล้ปิดตลาด (< 60 นาที) — ไม่เปิด Order',\n","    'GAP_WAIT':   '⏳ Gap เปิดตลาด > 20%R55 — รอ 80 แท่ง',\n","}\n","\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Signal Formatter\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","def fmt_sig(sig, t):\n","    d = sig.direction; pat = sig.pattern; det = sig.details\n","    ic = '🏔' if 'MOUNTAIN' in pat else ('📈' if d == 'BUY' else '📉')\n","    dt = 'BUY  ↑' if d == 'BUY' else 'SELL ↓'\n","    W = 66\n","    def row(k, v):\n","        k_str = str(k); v_str = str(v)\n","        pad = W - 4 - len(k_str) - 2 - len(v_str)\n","        return '║  ' + k_str + ': ' + v_str + ' '*max(0,pad) + '║'\n","    def sep(c='═'): return '╠' + c*W + '╣'\n","    title_body = ic + '  สัญญาณ ' + dt + '  ' + sig.quality + '  เวลา: ' + t\n","    pad_t = W - len(title_body.encode('utf-8')) + len(title_body)\n","    ln = [\n","        '',\n","        '╔' + '═'*W + '╗',\n","        '║  ' + title_body + ' '*(max(0, W - len(title_body) - 2)) + '║',\n","        sep(),\n","        row('ลักษณะกราฟ', PTH.get(pat, pat)),\n","        row('เทคนิค', TCH.get(pat, '')),\n","        sep(),\n","        row('Range 55', f\"{sig.R55:.0f} pip\"),\n","    ]\n","    if pat in ('MOUNTAIN','MOUNTAIN_R2'):\n","        hp = det['height']/sig.R55*100\n","        ln += [\n","            row('ฐานภูเขา', det['base']['times'][0]+'–'+det['base']['times'][-1]+'  '+f\"{det['base_lo']:.3f}\"),\n","            row('ยอดภูเขา', det['peak']['times'][0]+'–'+det['peak']['times'][-1]+'  '+f\"{det['peak_hi']:.3f}\"),\n","            row('ความสูง',  f\"{det['height']:.0f}pip ({hp:.1f}%R55) ห่างยอด {det['bars_since_peak']} แท่ง\"),\n","        ]\n","    elif 'lh' in det:\n","        ln += [\n","            row('LH (แนวต้าน)', det['lh']['times'][0]+'–'+det['lh']['times'][-1]+f\"  {det['lh_hi']:.3f}  ({det['pct_lh']:.1f}%)\"),\n","            row('LL (เป้าหมาย)', det['ll']['times'][0]+'–'+det['ll']['times'][-1]+f\"  {det['ll_lo']:.3f}  ({det['pct_ll']:.1f}%)\"),\n","            row('Rebound',  f\"{det['rebound']:.1f}%  (10–40%)\"),\n","        ]\n","    elif 'hl' in det:\n","        ln += [\n","            row('HL (แนวรับ)',   det['hl']['times'][0]+'–'+det['hl']['times'][-1]+f\"  {det['hl_lo']:.3f}  ({det['pct_hl']:.1f}%)\"),\n","            row('HH (เป้าหมาย)', det['hh']['times'][0]+'–'+det['hh']['times'][-1]+f\"  {det['hh_hi']:.3f}  ({det['pct_hh']:.1f}%)\"),\n","            row('Rebound',  f\"{det['rebound']:.1f}%  (10–40%)\"),\n","        ]\n","    sl_p = abs(sig.sl - sig.entry)*100; tp_p = abs(sig.tp_order - sig.entry)*100\n","    ln += [\n","        sep(), row('Entry', f\"{sig.entry:.3f}\"),\n","        row(f\"SL ({sig.sl_name})\", f\"{sig.sl:.3f}  │  {sl_p:.0f} pip\"),\n","        row(f\"TP ({sig.tp_name})\", f\"{sig.tp_order:.3f}  │  {tp_p:.0f} pip (order)\"),\n","        row('TP ref', f\"{sig.tp_ref:.3f}  │  {abs(sig.tp_ref-sig.entry)*100:.0f} pip\"),\n","        sep(),\n","        row('R:R', f\"{sig.rr:.2f}  {'✅' if sig.rr>=1.0 else '❌'}\"),\n","        row('Lot', f\"{sig.lot:.2f}\"),\n","        sep(),\n","        '║  TP Options:' + ' '*(W-12) + '║',\n","    ]\n","    for tl, tk in [('TP1','tp1'),('TP2','tp2'),('TP3','tp3_ref')]:\n","        v = det.get(tk)\n","        if v is None: continue\n","        dist = abs(v-sig.entry)*100; rr = dist/sig.risk_pip if sig.risk_pip else 0\n","        ch = ' ← เลือก' if tl in sig.tp_name else ''\n","        ok = '✅' if rr>=1.0 else '  '\n","        line = f\"║    {tl}: {v:.3f}  {dist:>7.0f}pip  R:R={rr:.2f} {ok}{ch}\"\n","        ln.append(line + ' '*(max(0, W+2-len(line.encode('utf-8'))+len(line))) + '║')\n","    ln.append('╚' + '═'*W + '╝')\n","    return '\\n'.join(ln)\n","\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Backtest Engine\n","# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n","# Config กฎเวลาและ Gap\n","\n","def _resolve_sl_tp_trail(direction, bar_open, bar_close, bar_high, bar_low, effective_sl, tp, is_entry_bar=False):\n","    \"\"\"\n","    Trailing SL version:\n","    - แท่งที่ขยับ SL: ถ้าโดนทั้ง SL และ TP3 → TP ชนะ\n","    - แท่งถัดไป: ถ้า low < SL → SL\n","    \"\"\"\n","    d=direction\n","    hit_sl = (bar_low <= effective_sl) if d=='BUY' else (bar_high >= effective_sl)\n","    hit_tp = (bar_high >= tp) if d=='BUY' else (bar_low <= tp)\n","    if hit_tp: return 'TP'   # TP ชนะเสมอถ้าโดนในแท่งเดียวกัน\n","    if hit_sl: return 'SL'\n","    return None\n","\n","def _resolve_sl_tp(direction: str, bar_open: float, bar_close: float,\n","                   bar_high: float, bar_low: float,\n","                   sl: float, tp: float,\n","                   is_entry_bar: bool = False) -> str:\n","    \"\"\"\n","    ตัดสิน SL หรือ TP โดนก่อน\n","\n","    ── โดนอย่างเดียว — แท่ง Entry ──────────────────────────\n","    BUY:\n","      แดง  + TP only → None (รอแท่งถัดไป)  ← ราคาอาจลงก่อนแล้วแค่ไส้บนแตะ\n","      เขียว + TP only → TP\n","      แดง  + SL only → SL\n","      เขียว + SL only → SL\n","\n","    SELL:\n","      แดง  + TP only → TP\n","      เขียว + TP only → None (รอแท่งถัดไป)  ← ราคาอาจขึ้นก่อนแล้วแค่ไส้ล่างแตะ\n","      แดง  + SL only → SL\n","      เขียว + SL only → SL\n","\n","    ── โดนอย่างเดียว — แท่งถัดไป ───────────────────────────\n","      คืนทันที ไม่ดูสี\n","\n","    ── โดนทั้งคู่ ────────────────────────────────────────────\n","    แท่ง Entry — ทั้ง BUY และ SELL:\n","      แดง   → SL\n","      เขียว → TP\n","\n","    แท่งถัดไป:\n","      BUY:  แดง → TP  | เขียว → SL\n","      SELL: แดง → SL  | เขียว → TP\n","\n","    คืน 'SL', 'TP', หรือ None (รอแท่งถัดไป)\n","    \"\"\"\n","    is_bullish = bar_close > bar_open\n","\n","    hit_sl = (direction == 'BUY'  and bar_low  <= sl) or \\\n","             (direction == 'SELL' and bar_high >= sl)\n","    hit_tp = (direction == 'BUY'  and bar_high >= tp) or \\\n","             (direction == 'SELL' and bar_low  <= tp)\n","\n","    if not hit_sl and not hit_tp:\n","        return None\n","\n","    # ── โดนอย่างเดียว ──────────────────────────────────────\n","    if hit_tp and not hit_sl:\n","        if is_entry_bar:\n","            # BUY แดง + TP only → รอ (ราคาอาจลงก่อน ไส้บนแค่แตะ)\n","            # BUY เขียว + TP only → TP\n","            # SELL แดง + TP only → TP\n","            # SELL เขียว + TP only → รอ (ราคาอาจขึ้นก่อน ไส้ล่างแค่แตะ)\n","            if direction == 'BUY':\n","                return None if not is_bullish else 'TP'\n","            else:  # SELL\n","                return None if is_bullish else 'TP'\n","        return 'TP'   # แท่งถัดไป → TP ทันที\n","\n","    if hit_sl and not hit_tp:\n","        if is_entry_bar:\n","            return 'SL'   # SL only ทุกสี ทุกทิศ = SL\n","        return 'SL'   # แท่งถัดไป → SL ทันที\n","\n","    # ── โดนทั้งคู่ → ดูสีแท่ง ─────────────────────────────\n","    if is_entry_bar:\n","        return 'SL' if not is_bullish else 'TP'\n","    else:\n","        if direction == 'BUY':\n","            return 'TP' if not is_bullish else 'SL'\n","        else:\n","            return 'SL' if not is_bullish else 'TP'\n","\n","MARKET_CLOSE_HOUR = 21\n","MARKET_CLOSE_MIN  = 0\n","NO_TRADE_MINS     = 120  # ห้ามเปิดก่อนตลาดปิด 2 ชั่วโมง (วันศุกร์เท่านั้น)\n","GAP_THRESHOLD_PCT = 0.20\n","GAP_WAIT_BARS     = 80\n","\n","\n","def run_backtest(bars, portfolio=1000.0, verbose=True):\n","    init_portfolio = portfolio  # ยึดเงินทุนเริ่มต้นคงที่\n","    in_trade = False; position = None\n","    trades = []; no = 0\n","    used_trend_bars:   set = set()   # bar_num LH/HL ที่ใช้ใน Trend\n","    used_mountain_bars: set = set()  # bar_num base_sw ที่ใช้ใน Mountain\n","\n","    # ── กฎ Gap เปิดตลาด ──────────────────────────────────\n","    gap_start_idx = 0\n","    if len(bars) >= 55:\n","        r55_init = (max(b.high for b in bars[:55]) - min(b.low for b in bars[:55])) * 100\n","        gap_start_idx = check_opening_gap(bars, r55_init, GAP_THRESHOLD_PCT, GAP_WAIT_BARS)\n","    start_idx = max(55, gap_start_idx)\n","    if gap_start_idx > 55 and verbose:\n","        print(f\"⚠️  Gap ราคา > 20%R55 — รอ {GAP_WAIT_BARS} แท่ง เริ่มเทรดที่ bar {gap_start_idx}\")\n","\n","    for i in range(start_idx, len(bars)+1):\n","        cur = bars[:i][-1]\n","        if in_trade:\n","            p = position; dr = p['dir']\n","            # ── Trailing SL v1: TP1→tech, TP2→TP1 ──────────────\n","            _stage_before = p.get('trail_stage', 0)\n","            try:\n","                _tech = float(p.get('tech_price') or p['sl'])\n","                _h = float(p.get('height_pip', 0) or 0)\n","                if p.get('trail_stage',0)==0 and p.get('tp1') and cur.high>=float(p['tp1']):\n","                    p['trail_sl'] = _tech + (_h * 0.05) / 100  # tech + 5%H\n","                    p['trail_stage'] = 1\n","                    p['trail_log'].append(f'TP1→SL=tech+5%H {p[\"trail_sl\"]:.3f}')\n","                _tp2_trail = p.get('tp2_base') or p.get('tp2')\n","                if p.get('trail_stage',0)==1 and _tp2_trail and cur.high>=float(_tp2_trail):\n","                    p['trail_sl'] = float(p['tp1'])\n","                    p['trail_stage'] = 2\n","                    p['trail_log'].append(f'TP2→SL=TP1 {p[\"trail_sl\"]:.3f}')\n","                # TP2+20%H → SL = TP2\n","                _tp2v = float(_tp2_trail) if _tp2_trail else 0\n","                _tp2_plus20 = _tp2v + (_h * 0.20) / 100 if _tp2v else 0\n","                if p.get('trail_stage',0)==2 and _tp2_plus20 and cur.high>=_tp2_plus20:\n","                    p['trail_sl'] = _tp2v\n","                    p['trail_stage'] = 3\n","                    p['trail_log'].append(f'TP2+20%H→SL=TP2 {p[\"trail_sl\"]:.3f}')\n","            except: pass\n","            _eff_sl = p['trail_sl'] if p.get('trail_sl') is not None else p['sl']\n","            _trail_moved = p.get('trail_stage',0) > _stage_before  # ขยับ TL ในแท่งนี้\n","            # ถ้าขยับ TL ในแท่งนี้ → ไม่ตรวจ SL ในแท่งเดียวกัน รอแท่งถัดไป\n","            if _trail_moved:\n","                outcome = 'TP' if cur.high >= p['tp'] else None  # ตรวจแค่ TP3\n","            else:\n","                outcome = _resolve_sl_tp_trail(dr, cur.open, cur.close, cur.high, cur.low, _eff_sl, p['tp'], is_entry_bar=False)\n","            # PnL ใช้ effective SL\n","            _exit_sl = _eff_sl\n","            if outcome == 'SL':\n","                # Trailing SL อาจอยู่เหนือ entry → pnl อาจเป็นบวก\n","                if p['dir'] == 'BUY':\n","                    pnl_pip = (_exit_sl - p['entry']) * 100\n","                else:\n","                    pnl_pip = (p['entry'] - _exit_sl) * 100\n","                pnl_usd = pnl_pip * p['lot']\n","                _date = cur.time[:10] if len(cur.time) >= 10 else cur.time\n","                # ── Post-SL depth: scan หา min low จากจุดเทคนิค ─────\n","                post_sl_h_pct = None\n","                _tp_s = str(p.get('tech_price','') or '')\n","                _hp_s = float(p.get('height_pip',0) or 0)\n","                if 'MOUNTAIN' in p.get('pattern','') and _tp_s and _tp_s not in ('','None') and _hp_s > 0:\n","                    _tec = float(_tp_s)\n","                    _lim = _tec - (_hp_s * 2.0) / 100   # 200% limit\n","                    _mlo = cur.low\n","                    for _fb in bars[i:]:\n","                        _mlo = min(_mlo, _fb.low)\n","                        if _fb.high >= _tec:\n","                            break  # ราคากลับถึงจุดเทคนิคแล้ว หยุดสแกน\n","                        if _fb.low <= _lim:\n","                            _mlo = _lim; break  # หยุดที่ 200%\n","                    _depth_pip = max(0.0, (_tec - _mlo) * 100)\n","                    post_sl_h_pct = min(_depth_pip / _hp_s * 100, 200.0)\n","                _result = 'WIN' if pnl_pip >= 0 else 'LOSS'\n","                _tp_name_used = p.get('tp_name','')\n","                if p.get('trail_sl') is not None:\n","                    _s = p.get('trail_stage',0)\n","                    _tp_name_used = 'TL-TP2+20%' if _s>=3 else ('TL-TP2' if _s>=2 else 'TL-TP1')\n","                trades.append({'result':_result,'open_time':p['open_time'],'close_time':cur.time,\n","                               'date':_date,'pnl':pnl_pip,'pnl_usd':pnl_usd,'lot':p['lot'],\n","                               'pattern':p['pattern'],'entry':p['entry'],'sl':p['sl'],'tp':p['tp'],\n","                               'same_bar':False,'tech_price':p.get('tech_price',''),'tech_time':p.get('tech_time',''),\n","                               'sl_name':p.get('sl_name',''),'tp_name':_tp_name_used,\n","                               'tp1':p.get('tp1',''),'tp2':p.get('tp2',''),'tp3':p.get('tp3',''),\n","                               'tp2_base':p.get('tp2_base',''),\n","                               'tp2_adj':p.get('tp2_adj',None),\n","                               'sl_all':p.get('sl_all',{}),\n","                               'sl_adjusted':p.get('sl_adjusted',None),\n","                               'height_pip':p.get('height_pip',0),'r55_entry':p.get('r55_entry',0),\n","                               'reached_tp2':False,'reached_tp3':False,'mae_h_pct':None,\n","                               'post_sl_h_pct':post_sl_h_pct,'trail_log':' | '.join(p.get('trail_log',[])),\n","                               'actual_exit':round(_exit_sl, 3)})\n","                _exit_label = 'TL โดน' if p.get('trail_sl') is not None else 'SL โดน'\n","                _exit_icon  = '⚡' if p.get('trail_sl') is not None else '❌'\n","                if verbose:\n","                    print(f\"\\n  {_exit_icon} {_exit_label}  {cur.time}  P&L = {pnl_pip:+.0f} pip  ({pnl_usd:+.2f} USD)  Lot={p['lot']:.2f}\")\n","                # ── Counter SELL เฉพาะเมื่อโดน SL ปกติ (ไม่ใช่ TL) ──\n","                _is_tl = p.get('trail_sl') is not None\n","                _tech_p_cs = float(p.get('tech_price') or 0)\n","                _h_cs = float(p.get('height_pip', 0) or 0)\n","                if False and 'MOUNTAIN' in p.get('pattern','') and _tech_p_cs > 0 and _h_cs > 0 and not _is_tl:  # ปิดชั่วคราว\n","                    _cs_entry  = _exit_sl\n","                    _cs_sl     = _tech_p_cs              # SL = จุดเทคนิค\n","                    _cs_tp     = _tech_p_cs - _h_cs/100  # TP = base_lo − 100%H\n","                    _cs_risk   = abs(_cs_entry - _cs_sl)*100\n","                    _cs_reward = abs(_cs_entry - _cs_tp)*100\n","                    if _cs_risk > 0 and _cs_tp < _cs_entry:  # SELL ต้อง TP < entry\n","                        _cs_lot  = round((portfolio*0.10)/_cs_risk, 2)\n","                        _cs_rr   = _cs_reward/_cs_risk\n","                        # ตั้ง counter position\n","                        position = {\n","                            'dir':'SELL','pattern':'MOUNTAIN_COUNTER','entry':_cs_entry,\n","                            'sl':_cs_sl,'tp':_cs_tp,'entry_bar':cur.bar_num,\n","                            'open_time':cur.time,'base_lo':_tech_p_cs,\n","                            'lot':_cs_lot,'tech_price':str(_tech_p_cs),'tech_time':p.get('tech_time',''),\n","                            'sl_name':'SL=tech','tp_name':'TP=100%H',\n","                            'tp1':'','tp2':'','tp3':str(_cs_tp),\n","                            'tp2_base':_cs_tp,'tp2_adj':None,\n","                            'sl_all':{'SL1':round(_cs_sl,3),'SL2':'','SL3':'','SL3.5':''},\n","                            'sl_adjusted':None,\n","                            'height_pip':_h_cs,'r55_entry':p.get('r55_entry',0),\n","                            'trail_sl':None,'trail_stage':0,'trail_log':[]}\n","                        in_trade = True\n","                        if verbose:\n","                            print(f\"     🔄 Counter SELL: entry={_cs_entry:.3f} SL={_cs_sl:.3f} TP={_cs_tp:.3f} R:R={_cs_rr:.2f}\")\n","                        continue\n","                in_trade=False; position=None; continue\n","            if outcome == 'TP':\n","                pnl_pip = abs(p['tp']-p['entry'])*100\n","                pnl_usd = pnl_pip * p['lot']\n","                _date = cur.time[:10] if len(cur.time) >= 10 else cur.time\n","                # actual exit = TP3 order price (genuine TP hit)\n","                _actual_exit = p['tp']\n","                trades.append({'result':'WIN','open_time':p['open_time'],'close_time':cur.time,\n","                               'date':_date,'pnl':pnl_pip,'pnl_usd':pnl_usd,'lot':p['lot'],\n","                               'pattern':p['pattern'],'entry':p['entry'],'sl':p['sl'],'tp':p['tp'],\n","                               'actual_exit':_actual_exit,\n","                               'same_bar':False,'tech_price':p.get('tech_price',''),'tech_time':p.get('tech_time',''),\n","                               'sl_name':p.get('sl_name',''),'tp_name':p.get('tp_name',''),\n","                               'tp1':p.get('tp1',''),'tp2':p.get('tp2',''),'tp3':p.get('tp3',''),\n","                               'tp2_base':p.get('tp2_base',''),\n","                               'tp2_adj':p.get('tp2_adj',None),\n","                               'sl_all':p.get('sl_all',{}),\n","                               'sl_adjusted':p.get('sl_adjusted',None),\n","                               'height_pip':p.get('height_pip',0),'r55_entry':p.get('r55_entry',0)})\n","                # ── MAE: low สุดก่อนถึง TP (% ของความสูงภูเขา) ───────\n","                mae_h_pct = None\n","                if 'MOUNTAIN' in p.get('pattern','') and p.get('tech_price') and p.get('height_pip',0):\n","                    _tp_str2 = str(p.get('tech_price','') or '')\n","                    if _tp_str2 and _tp_str2 not in ('','None'):\n","                        _tech_p2 = float(_tp_str2)\n","                        _h2      = float(p['height_pip'])\n","                        _eb2     = p.get('entry_bar', i)\n","                        _eidx2   = next((k for k,b in enumerate(bars) if b.bar_num == _eb2), 0)\n","                        _scan2   = bars[_eidx2:i+1]  # entry → TP bar\n","                        _limit2  = _tech_p2 - (_h2 * 2) / 100  # 2×height limit\n","                        _mae_lo2 = _tech_p2  # เริ่มจากจุดเทคนิค\n","                        for _fb2 in _scan2:\n","                            _mae_lo2 = min(_mae_lo2, _fb2.low)\n","                            if _fb2.low <= _limit2:\n","                                _mae_lo2 = _limit2  # cap ที่ 2×height\n","                                break\n","                        _mae_pip2 = max(0.0, (_tech_p2 - _mae_lo2) * 100)\n","                        mae_h_pct = _mae_pip2 / _h2 * 100 if _h2 > 0 else 0\n","\n","                # ── ตรวจว่าราคาไปถึง TP2/TP3 ไหมหลัง TP โดน ────────\n","                reached_tp2 = False\n","                reached_tp3 = False\n","                if 'MOUNTAIN' in p.get('pattern',''):\n","                    _tp2_val = float(p.get('tp2_base') or 0)\n","                    _tp3_val = float(p.get('tp3') or 0)\n","                    _stop_low = p['entry'] - (p['tp']-p['entry'])  # หยุดถ้าลงเกินนี้\n","                    for fwd in bars[i:]:\n","                        if _tp2_val > 0 and fwd.high >= _tp2_val:\n","                            reached_tp2 = True\n","                        if _tp3_val > 0 and fwd.high >= _tp3_val:\n","                            reached_tp3 = True\n","                        if reached_tp2 and reached_tp3:\n","                            break\n","                        if fwd.low < _stop_low:\n","                            break\n","                # อัปเดต trade dict\n","                trades[-1]['reached_tp2'] = reached_tp2\n","                trades[-1]['reached_tp3'] = reached_tp3\n","                trades[-1]['mae_h_pct']   = mae_h_pct\n","                # ถ้า MAE ≥ 200%H → ถือว่าไม่กลับถึง TP\n","                if mae_h_pct is not None and mae_h_pct >= 200:\n","                    trades[-1]['reached_tp2'] = False\n","                    trades[-1]['reached_tp3'] = False\n","                if verbose:\n","                    print(f\"\\n  ✅ TP โดน  {cur.time}  P&L = +{pnl_pip:.0f} pip  (+{pnl_usd:.2f} USD)  Lot={p['lot']:.2f}\")\n","                    if reached_tp3: print(f\"     🎯 ราคาไปถึง TP3 หลัง TP โดน\")\n","                in_trade=False; position=None; continue\n","            continue\n","        sig = find_signal(bars[:i], portfolio=init_portfolio,\n"," wd_grp[wd]['loss_usd']+=t.get('pnl_usd',0)\n","\n","    print(f\"\\n{'═'*W}\\n  📅  Win Rate แยกตามวันในสัปดาห์\\n{'═'*W}\")\n","    print(f\"  {'วัน':<8}  {'ชนะ':>4}  {'แพ้':>4}  {'รวม':>4} 0\n","        net_p = g['win_pip']+g['loss_pip']\n","        net_u = g['win_usd']+g['loss_usd']\n","        net_p = round(net_p, 2); net_u = round(net_u, 2)\n","        icon = '✅' if net_u > 0 else {'─'*58}\")\n","    all_wd = [wd_grp[wd] for wd in range(5) if wd in wd_grp]\n","    if all_wd:\n","        avg_wr = sum(g['w']/(g['w']+g['l'])*100 for g in all_wd if g['w']+g['l']>0) / 00:00–09:00 UTC\n","    # London:      07:00–16:00 UTC  (overlap กับ Asian 07-09)\n","    # New York:    13:00–22:00 UTC  (overlap กับ London 13-16)\n","    def get_session(time_str):\n","        try:\n","            from datetime import datetime as _dt\n","            t = _dt.strptime(time_str[:16], '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')\n","            h = t.hour\n","            sessions = []\n","            if  0 <= h <  7: sessions.append('Asian')\n","            if  7 <= h <  9: sessions.append('Asian+London')\n","            if  9 <= h < 13: sessions.append('London')\n","            if 13 <= h < 16: sessions.append('London+NewYork')\n","            if 16 <= h < 22: sessions.append('New York')\n","            if 22 <= h < 24: sessions.append('After NY')\n","            return sessions[0] if sessions else 'Other'\n","        except:\n","            return 'Other'\n","\n","    sess_grp = defaultdict(lambda:{'w':0,'l':0,'win_pip':0.0,'loss_pip':0.0,'win_usd':0.0,'loss_usd':0.0})\n","    SESS_ORDER = ['Asian','Asian+London','London','London+NewYork','New York','After NY','Other']\n","    for t in trades:\n","        sess = get_session(t.get('open_time', t.get('close_time','')))\n"," sess_grp[sess]['l']+=1; sess_grp[sess]['loss_pip']+=t['pnl']; sess_grp[sess]['loss_usd']+=t.get('pnl_usd',0)\n","\n","    print(f\"\\n{'═'*W}\\n  🌏  Win Rate แยกตาม Session (UTC)\\n{'═'*W}\")\n","    print(f\"  {'Session':<16}  {'เวลา UTC':<14}  {'ชนะ':>4}  {'แพ้':>4}  {'WR%':>6}  {'Net pip':>9}  {'Net USD':>9}\")\n","    print(f\"  {'─'*72}\")\n","    SESS_TIME = {\n","        'Asian':          '00:00–07:00',\n","        'Asian+London':   '07:00–09:00',\n","        'London':         '09:00–13:00',\n","        'London+NewYork': '13:00–16:00',\n","        'New York':       '16:00–22:00',\n","        'After NY':       '22:00–24:00',\n","        'Other':          '—',\n","    }\n","    for sess in SESS_ORDER:\n","        g = sess_grp.get(sess)\n","        if g is None: continue\n","        total_t = g['w']+g['l']\n","        wr = g['w']/total_t*100 if total_t else 0\n","        net_p = g['win_pip']+g['loss_pip']\n","        net_u = g['win_usd']+g['loss_usd']\n","        net_p = round(net_p, 2); net_u = round(net_u, 2)\n","        icon = '✅' if net_u > 0 else ('❌' if net_u < 0 else '➖')\n","        print(f\"  {icon} {sess:<14}  {SESS_TIME.get(sess,'—'):<14}  {g['w']:>4} ('140-160%H', lambda p: 140 <= p < 160),\n","            ('160-180%H', lambda p: 160 <= p < 180),\n","            ('180-200%H', lambda p: 180 <= p < 200),\n","            ('>200%H',    lambda p: p >= 200),\n","        ]\n","        total_win_mtn = len(win_mtn_mae)\n","        print(f\"\\n{'═'*W}\\n  📊  MAE ก่อน TP/SL (แบ่งตาม %ความสูงภูเขา)\\n{'═'*W}\")\n","        print(f\"  ไม้ Mountain (WIN+LOSS, มีข้อมูล) : {total_win_mtn} ไม้\")\n","        print(f\"  {'─'*W}\")\n","        print(f\"  {'bracket':<13}  {'รวม(%)':>9}  {'W/L':>7}  {'TP2only(%)':>13}  {'TP3(%)':>12}\")\n","        print(f\"  {'─'*W}\")\n","        for lbl, fn in brackets_mae:\n","            grp = [t for t in win_mtn_mae if fn(t['mae_h_pct'])]\n","            if not grp: continue\n","            n   = len(grp)\n","            nw  = sum(1 for t in grp if t['result']=='WIN')\n","            nl  = n - nw\n","            n3  = sum(1 for t in grp if t['result']=='WIN' and t.get('reached_tp3'))\n"," dict, csv_filename: str, version: str = \"v4.53\"):\n","    \"\"\"\n","    สร้าง Google Sheet สรุปผล Backtest\n","    ชื่อ Sheet: {csv_filename} Backtest {version}\n","    \"\"\"\n","    try:\n","        from googleapiclient.discovery import build\n","        import re\n","\n","        # ── Auth สำหรับ Colab ─────────────────────────────\n","        from google.colab import auth as _colab_auth\n"," net_pip    = summary['net_pip']\n","\n","        WEEKDAY_TH = {0:'จันทร์',1:'อังคาร',2:'พุธ',3:'พฤหัส',4:'ศุกร์'}\n","        SESS_TIME  = {\n","  }\n","\n","        def get_wd(d):\n","            try:\n","                from datetime import datetime as _dt\n","                return _dt.strptime(d,'%Y-%m-%d').weekday()\n","            except: [['วันเปิด','เวลาเปิด','วันปิด','เวลาปิด',\n","                  'Entry','Exit','Lot','pip','USD','R:R','ลักษณะกราฟ','Same-bar','จุดเทคนิค','เวลาจุดเทคนิค',\n","                  'TP1','TP2','TP2 Adj','TP3','SL1','SL2','SL3','SL3.5','SL Adj','Trailing SL','MAE %Height','Depth หลัง SL (%H)']]\n","        rows1 = []\n","        for t in trades:\n","            exit_price = t.get('actual_exit', if len(ot)>=16 else ''\n","            cd = ct[:10] if len(ct)>=10 else ct\n","            cw = ct[11:16] if len(ct)>=16 else ''\n","            risk = abs(t['entry']-t['sl'])*100\n","            # R:R จริง: reward = pnl จริง / risk เดิม (SL 40%H)\n","            # ถ้า LOSS: reward = TP3 ที่ตั้งใจ / risk (แสดงศักยภาพ)\n","            if t['result'] in ('WIN',) or t.get('trail_log'):\n"," import defaultdict as _dd\n","        _dg = _dd(lambda:{'w':0,'l':0,'net':0.0})\n","        for _t in trades:\n","            _d = _t.get('date','')[:10]\n","            if not _d: continue\n"," mws=mls=cws=cls=0; mwd=mld=cwd=cld=0\n","        mwusd=mlusd=cwusd=clusd=0.0\n","        for t in trades:\n","            u=t.get('pnl_usd',0)\n","            if t['result']=='WIN':\n","         cls+=1;cws=0;mls=max(mls,cls)\n","                cld+=1;cwd=max(0,cwd-1);mld=max(mld,cld)\n","                clusd+=abs(u);cwusd=max(0.0,cwusd-abs(u));mlusd=max(mlusd,clusd)\n","\n","        rows2 = [\n","            ['สรุปรวม','',''],\n","            ['รายการ','pip','USD'],\n","            ['เทรดทั้งหมด',summary['n_trades'],''],\n","            ['ชนะ',summary['n_wins'],''],\n"," _brackets_g=[('<10%H',0,10),('10-20%H',10,20),('20-30%H',20,30),('30-40%H',30,40),\n","                         ('40-50%H',40,50),('50-60%H',50,60),('60-70%H',60,70),('70-80%H',70,80),\n"," ('140-160%H',140,160),('160-180%H',160,180),('180-200%H',180,200),('>200%H',200,9999)]\n","            _twm=len(_wmae)\n","            rows2.extend([['','','','','',''],\n"," _g=[t for t in _wmae if _lo<=t['mae_h_pct']<_hi]\n","                if not _g: continue\n","                _nw=sum(1 for t in _g if t['result']==\"WIN\")\n","                _nl=len(_g)-_nw\n","
